# AI Media Scout — 2026-10-28

Expanded research covers 73 creative fields through 591 repository queries and 18 model tasks, adding generative gameworld searches. This edition contains 51 full profiles and 5 screened leads, including completed guides for earlier entries and a substantive ComfyUI v0.43 update. Every admitted software license was read in full; weights, paid services and proprietary hosts are separate. All 1,236 entries present at the start of this run already have current weekly reviews; today’s records also receive dated six-check assessments. Quality labels express evidence, not popularity. No discovered app was installed or run. Query failures, bounded pagination and blocked ecosystems remain visible; the findings are broad but not an exhaustive inventory. All 591 repository queries and 18 model-task queries succeeded; 115 repository searches were bounded. Twelve distinct watchlist repository requests returned 404 and one additional commit lookup returned 409, separately from search success. SubGenPlayer now has an unavailable-source assessment; SNAC Go’s current official Forgejo source was recovered and cited without duplicating the tool. Profile breakdown: 42 first additions, 8 completed earlier leads and 1 substantive update; plus 5 new screened leads.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

## Search and review counts

73 creative fields; 591/591 repository queries attempted; 18/18 model-task queries attempted. 26812 distinct source candidates and 856 model leads. 51 detailed profiles and 5 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 0 failed repository queries, 0 partial repository queries, 115 bounded repository queries, 0 failed model-task queries and 4 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · gap: Live GitLab searches surfaced Brightbound, but README/license retrieval failed. Existing OpenPixels resurfaced without a new qualifying change. No additional GitLab tool cleared full eligibility review. [Source](https://gitlab.com/Roxanne_Ardary/brightbound)
- **codeberg** · gap: The targeted live source-group query was blocked by robots/access restrictions. No restriction was bypassed; absence of results does not establish absence of useful tools.
- **sourcehut** · gap: The targeted live source-group query was blocked by robots/access restrictions; this remains an explicit coverage gap.
- **packages** · gap: Targeted npm CreatPPT returned 403 and PyPI visage-image could not be retrieved. Copisteria package search returned an unrelated Copier result. Package-host verification remains incomplete; official repositories were reviewed separately. [Source](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@seekskyworld/creatppt) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/visage-image/)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Reviewed ComfyUI v0.43, FoleyCrafter/AnimaFlow nodes, Fender Studio MCP and Grafana Flint integrations, including separate host and model obligations. [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/releases/tag/v0.43.0) [Source](https://github.com/maaatheeew/fender-studio-mcp)
- **project-sites** · searched: Searched primary creative/research sites across all 73 observed fields and read official Comfy CLI/SDXL guidance. Some MOSS and WebSfM site retrievals failed; repository documentation supplies separately archived evidence. [Source](https://docs.comfy.org/comfy-cli/getting-started) [Source](https://comfyanonymous.github.io/ComfyUI_examples/sdxl/) [Source](https://neuralworlds.net/)
- **research-code** · searched: Followed MOSS image, Woosh audio, NRP rendering, PASE speech and related primary papers/code. Research origin alone neither raises nor lowers a quality tier. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image) [Source](https://github.com/SonyResearch/Woosh) [Source](https://github.com/cisco-open/pase)
- **international** · searched: Reviewed Chinese DSH/Eva/music-mv/microcourse guides, German AuraOS, Turkish DubWorld and Russian Synaps documentation using the same licensing and evidence requirements. [Source](https://github.com/QinLuza/dsh-aisketch) [Source](https://github.com/Revisor01/auraos-moodlight) [Source](https://github.com/zavalishev/synaps-video-studio)
- **forgejo** · searched: Recovered the existing SNAC Go entry’s official Forgejo source after its GitHub metadata failed. Direct HTTP reads returned the current repository, full README and full Apache license; the library assessment now cites this host without duplicating the tool. [Source](https://git.tomfos.tr/tom/snac-go)

## MOSS-Image

Images & design · Typography, fonts & layout · Vector graphics, illustration & textures

A recently created project receiving its first full library guide; its creation is not proof of production readiness.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Posters, illustrated typography and concept images where words and composition need explicit placement.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-28. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README links image galleries, Base-versus-SFT comparisons, a hosted Space and reproducible prompt examples. These are author demonstrations, not our quality test. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Clone the source, create a Python virtual environment, install requirements, and obtain Base or SFT weights. For Gradio install the ui extra and launch gradio_app.py; ComfyUI offers a separate native integration. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Reviewed a bounded live sample of 10 commits, 0 release records and 10 issue/PR records. Latest sampled commit: 2026-10-27. Sampled support topics: Why is it qwen3.5-9b-instruct?; Community project: hosted MOSS-Image studio with a visual JSON layout builder (no ComfyUI needed). Activity is documented; sustained support and absence of workflow blockers are not established. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/commits/main) [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** The reviewed author examples and bounded support sample did not establish independent first-hand reproduction of this exact workflow. Broad live web research was supplementary; author demos and star counts do not count as independent validation.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The official Base/SFT model cards state Apache-2.0 for released weights and bundled inference code. Third-party adapters, alternate bundles and hosted services still require their own term checks. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Text and layout fidelity still need visual inspection. Mac MPS is explicitly experimental and changes process-wide MPS synchronization; that optional port does not make every MOSS workflow Experimental.

### How it uses AI

A diffusion pipeline generates images from long captions and structured text/object layouts, using a 5B image backbone, a Qwen text encoder and separate VAE. [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

### Introduction

A diffusion pipeline generates images from long captions and structured text/object layouts, using a 5B image backbone, a Qwen text encoder and separate VAE. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

### What it is good for

Posters, illustrated typography and concept images where words and composition need explicit placement. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

### Demo & examples

The README links image galleries, Base-versus-SFT comparisons, a hosted Space and reproducible prompt examples. These are author demonstrations, not our quality test. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

### Install

Clone the source, create a Python virtual environment, install requirements, and obtain Base or SFT weights. For Gradio install the ui extra and launch gradio_app.py; ComfyUI offers a separate native integration. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the source, create a Python virtual environment, install requirements, and obtain Base or SFT weights. For Gradio install the ui extra and launch gradio_app.py; ComfyUI offers a separate native integration.

```sh
python -m venv .venv
```


```sh
pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
pip install -e '.[ui]'
```


```sh
python gradio_app.py --model OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

1. Select Base or SFT and an appropriate memory mode.
2. Enter a caption, or JSON text/object boxes with coordinates matched to the canvas.
3. Start with one image, inspect spelling and composition, then change the prompt or seed.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

- **Hardware:** CUDA minimum VRAM/RAM is not specified in the README. The optional Mac guide records M1 Max/32 GB and about 40 GB free disk for one model/environment; this is a recorded configuration, not a universal minimum.
- **Software:** Official model card requires Python 3.10+, PyTorch 2.9+, diffusers 0.38+, transformers 5.3+ and matching dependencies; optional Gradio. Mac route documents native arm64 Python 3.12 and pinned dependencies.
- **Platforms:** CUDA route documented; optional Apple-silicon MPS route tested by its contributor on M1 Max/32 GB only. No general Windows/Linux/Mac compatibility guarantee.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 5](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

- **Code:** Apache-2.0
- **Weights:** The official Base/SFT model cards state Apache-2.0 for released weights and bundled inference code. Third-party adapters, alternate bundles and hosted services still require their own term checks.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows Apache-2.0. The official Base/SFT model cards state Apache-2.0 for released weights and bundled inference code. Third-party adapters, alternate bundles and hosted services still require their own term checks.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

### Limitations

Text and layout fidelity still need visual inspection. Mac MPS is explicitly experimental and changes process-wide MPS synchronization; that optional port does not make every MOSS workflow Experimental. [Source 1](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-Image/blob/main/docs/macos.md) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-Base) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/OpenMOSS-Team/MOSS-Image-SFT)

## WebSfM

Photogrammetry, scanning & neural rendering · 3D, reconstruction & assets · Browser tools & web media · AI cartography and artistic maps

First full guide in this library. This is a newly reviewed discovery, not a claim that the software launched today.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Turning overlapping location or object photos into point clouds and meshes for spatial art and scene capture.

- **Creative results · documented:** The README includes a rotating reconstruction, orthophoto and a 42-photo example project, plus author benchmark results with explicit accuracy gaps. [Source](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://websfm.app)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use websfm.app or clone the repository and install the Node dependencies. The compiled WASM is included; choose learned backends and approve their separate model downloads. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://websfm.app)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Reviewed a bounded live sample of 10 commits, 3 release records and 10 issue/PR records. Latest sampled commit: 2026-10-28. Sampled support topics: Automatic masking by image content; Panoramic satellite cameras: model the motion during the scan. Activity is documented; sustained support and absence of workflow blockers are not established. Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The maintainer labels the entire application beta; learned-detector interactive paths, exported-file round trips and portability are not yet verified.

### How it uses AI

Browser photogrammetry offers learned ALIKED, DISK or SuperPoint detection and LightGlue matching alongside classical reconstruction. [Source](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://websfm.app)

### Introduction

Browser photogrammetry offers learned ALIKED, DISK or SuperPoint detection and LightGlue matching alongside classical reconstruction. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

### What it is good for

Turning overlapping location or object photos into point clouds and meshes for spatial art and scene capture. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

### Demo & examples

The README includes a rotating reconstruction, orthophoto and a 42-photo example project, plus author benchmark results with explicit accuracy gaps. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

### Install

Use websfm.app or clone the repository and install the Node dependencies. The compiled WASM is included; choose learned backends and approve their separate model downloads. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use websfm.app or clone the repository and install the Node dependencies. The compiled WASM is included; choose learned backends and approve their separate model downloads.

```sh
npm install
```


```sh
npm run fetch:examples
```


```sh
npm run dev
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

1. Open the example or import overlapping photos.
2. Select a learned detector/matcher, reconstruct cameras and inspect the match graph.
3. Generate the required dense product and verify exported geometry in the destination software.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

- **Hardware:** No system RAM or VRAM minimum stated. Current WASM memory limit is 4 GB; this is an address-space ceiling, not required system RAM.
- **Software:** Node 22 recommended for source development, minimum 20.19; Chromium recommended, OPFS local storage and optional WebGPU.
- **Platforms:** Browser application; Chromium is the exercised path. Firefox/Safari and other GPUs remain explicitly unverified, not inferred operating-system support.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://websfm.app)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** Learned detector/matcher weights download separately and retain their own terms. Example drone photos are CC0.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Learned detector/matcher weights download separately and retain their own terms. Example drone photos are CC0.
- **Cost:** Local compute; no required paid inference service documented.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

### Limitations

The maintainer labels the entire application beta; learned-detector interactive paths, exported-file round trips and portability are not yet verified. [Source 1](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://websfm.app)

- [Documentation](https://github.com/fdahle/websfm/blob/main/README.md)

First full guide in this library. This is a newly reviewed discovery, not a claim that the software launched today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Scope: Editing selected moments, adding animated overlays and exporting reproducible short edits under agent direction.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-28. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** An official ten-second explainer includes source, audio, preview and an MCP project-generation script. [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Download the official Windows or Linux ZIP and checksum, extract it, and follow its platform installer. Launch OpenEdit.cmd or OpenEdit.sh, then merge the generated MCP entry into the chosen agent host. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Reviewed a bounded live sample of 10 commits, 2 release records and 10 issue/PR records. Latest sampled commit: 2026-10-17. Sampled support topics: Render fails silently when melt is missing and crashes Windows console with UnicodeEncodeError. Activity is documented; sustained support and absence of workflow blockers are not established. [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/commits/main) [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** The reviewed author examples and bounded support sample did not establish independent first-hand reproduction of this exact workflow. Broad live web research was supplementary; author demos and star counts do not count as independent validation.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Original code MIT; vendored Diffusion MPL-2.0, fonts OFL, downloaded runtimes and optional legacy Remotion have separate terms. Agent provider credentials and costs belong to that host. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Installer self-tests and an author example do not establish independent creative use. Hardware-encoder and driver coverage is not universal.

### How it uses AI

A native video editor exposes timeline, graphics and render operations to an AI agent over MCP while the application controls frame timing. [Source](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A native video editor exposes timeline, graphics and render operations to an AI agent over MCP while the application controls frame timing. [Source 1](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)

Editing selected moments, adding animated overlays and exporting reproducible short edits under agent direction. [Source 1](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)

Download the official Windows or Linux ZIP and checksum, extract it, and follow its platform installer. Launch OpenEdit.cmd or OpenEdit.sh, then merge the generated MCP entry into the chosen agent host. [Source 1](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Download the official Windows or Linux ZIP and checksum, extract it, and follow its platform installer. Launch OpenEdit.cmd or OpenEdit.sh, then merge the generated MCP entry into the chosen agent host.

```sh
open_edit init /absolute/path/to/your-project
```


```sh
open_edit mcp-config --project /absolute/path/to/your-project
```


```sh
open_edit doctor
```

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/AH64-dll/OpenEdit/blob/main/README.md)

3. Review the preview, render the final output and reopen the saved project.

- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Original code MIT; vendored Diffusion MPL-2.0, fonts OFL, downloaded runtimes and optional legacy Remotion have separate terms. Agent provider credentials and costs belong to that host.

### How it uses AI

A Windows studio orchestrates local image/video models, story planning, cast references and shot assembly. [Source](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Windows studio orchestrates local image/video models, story planning, cast references and shot assembly. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Exploring short narrative films on one modest-VRAM PC with substantial system RAM. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The author publishes LTX-2.5 clips, prompts, timings and QA failures on one RTX 3070. Some takes were selected or re-prompted. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Extract the source to a short Windows path, run INSTALL.bat, select model components with install/INSTALL-MAIN.bat, then start.bat. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Extract the source to a short Windows path, run INSTALL.bat, select model components with install/INSTALL-MAIN.bat, then start.bat.

```sh
install\INSTALL-MAIN.bat
```


```sh
start.bat
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

1. Choose only models whose terms and hardware fit the project.
2. Create the story and cast references, render a short shot and inspect QA.
3. Assemble shots; inspect motion, seams and drift before exporting a longer film.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Tested RTX 3070 8 GB, 64 GB system RAM. Base installation about 17 GB before weights; model downloads range from small postprocessing files to tens of GB. Full reference model folders total about 211 GB.
- **Software:** Windows x64; installer supplies Python 3.12, pinned ComfyUI environments and FFmpeg. NVIDIA driver R580+ for LTX-2.5/H3, R560+ for other documented runtimes.
- **Platforms:** Windows 11 tested; Windows 10 1803+ untested. Main studio uses Win32 APIs; Linux worker/CI support is not desktop support.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT original code plus identified GPL files and separate fonts. Weights are not bundled. The README flags Qwen-Image 2.1 non-commercial terms, geographic restrictions for H3 and LTX revenue-dependent commercial terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT original code plus identified GPL files and separate fonts. Weights are not bundled. The README flags Qwen-Image 2.1 non-commercial terms, geographic restrictions for H3 and LTX revenue-dependent commercial terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute; optional model or commercial-use licensing and story-provider charges remain separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

One-machine evidence only; not real time. One project renders at once. Seamless chains are an experimental optional path with documented seam/drift failures. [Source 1](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Documentation](https://github.com/harlixay7/Kinoloom/blob/main/README.md)

The project links examples for 43 templates; independent quality and cost reproduction remain unverified. [Source 1](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openpromo.gg/docs/self-hosting) [Source 3](https://openpromo.gg/templates)

### Install

Clone the source, set local authentication/encryption settings from the example, then build the Docker Compose stack. Add the separately billed generation provider through the dashboard. [Source 1](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openpromo.gg/docs/self-hosting) [Source 3](https://openpromo.gg/templates)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the source, set local authentication/encryption settings from the example, then build the Docker Compose stack. Add the separately billed generation provider through the dashboard.

```sh
docker compose up -d --build
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openpromo.gg/docs/self-hosting) [Source 3](https://openpromo.gg/templates)

1. Review the extracted brand profile and source-backed claims.
2. Choose a template and generate a short draft within a spending limit.
3. Inspect imagery, text, voice and credits before exporting.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openpromo.gg/docs/self-hosting) [Source 3](https://openpromo.gg/templates)

- **Hardware:** At least 2 CPUs and 4 GB memory allocated for Chrome/FFmpeg rendering; disk and GPU minimums not stated.
- **Software:** Docker; source development uses Node 26+, pnpm 10 and Postgres 15+. Container includes Chrome and FFmpeg.
- **Platforms:** Docker self-hosting documented; no blanket host operating-system certification.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://openpromo.gg/docs/self-hosting) [Source 4](https://openpromo.gg/templates)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT application; required YepAPI generation uses separately licensed and billed model services. Rendering dependencies and fonts have separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT application; required YepAPI generation uses separately licensed and billed model services. Rendering dependencies and fonts have separate terms.
- **Cost:** Self-hosting plus required provider usage; published example prices are author estimates and vary by models and media.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/duckduckdeploy/openpromo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openpromo.gg/docs/self-hosting) [Source 3](https://openpromo.gg/templates)

The current project explicitly describes itself as early development. Budget estimates and template demonstrations are not independently validated. ## Aspromonte

Photogrammetry, scanning & neural rendering · Spatial audio & volumetric media · AI cartography and artistic maps

A recently created project receiving its first full library guide; its creation is not proof of production readiness.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Spatial documentation and navigable location studies, with explicit limits on off-path views and change detection.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-28. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Author imagery includes a trail splat and fly-through; the paper-style results report both successful detections and false positives. [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Prepare a Python environment, install requirements, CUDA-enabled COLMAP and ExifTool, and separately obtain Inria 3DGS for the splat route. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Reviewed a bounded live sample of 10 commits, 0 release records and 0 issue/PR records. Latest sampled commit: 2026-10-26. The sample did not establish independent support-resolution history. Activity is documented; sustained support and absence of workflow blockers are not established. [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/commits/main) [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** The reviewed author examples and bounded support sample did not establish independent first-hand reproduction of this exact workflow. Broad live web research was supplementary; author demos and star counts do not count as independent validation.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT wrapper; the required Inria 3DGS route is non-commercial, so this creative reconstruction workflow is not unrestricted. Other components and geospatial data retain their own licenses. Local compute and compatible capture hardware; model/code restrictions remain separate. [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. One 152 m trail with three passes; off-path splats smear and float. Height-change tests produced six false positives among eight detections.

### How it uses AI

A research pipeline turns walking GoPro footage into georeferenced Gaussian-splat scenes and compares repeated passes. [Source](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A research pipeline turns walking GoPro footage into georeferenced Gaussian-splat scenes and compares repeated passes. [Source 1](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)

Spatial documentation and navigable location studies, with explicit limits on off-path views and change detection. [Source 1](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)

2. Prepare a Python environment, install requirements, CUDA-enabled COLMAP and ExifTool, and separately obtain Inria 3DGS for the splat route.

```sh
python3 -m venv venv
```


```sh
venv/bin/pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
python3 run_pipeline.py --config pipeline.conf --video seg01.mp4 --workdir seg01
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/diego-barreca-2004/Project_Aspromonte/blob/main/README.md)

2. Reconstruct and inspect camera/georeference quality gates.
3. Train the optional splat route, inspect near-path views, and compare passes only with appropriate calibration data.

- **Weights:** MIT wrapper; the required Inria 3DGS route is non-commercial, so this creative reconstruction workflow is not unrestricted. Other components and geospatial data retain their own licenses.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT wrapper; the required Inria 3DGS route is non-commercial, so this creative reconstruction workflow is not unrestricted. Other components and geospatial data retain their own licenses.

A transcription, translation and voice pipeline overlays translated narration while retaining quieter original audio and subtitle tracks. [Source](https://github.com/vocnu/engine/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A transcription, translation and voice pipeline overlays translated narration while retaining quieter original audio and subtitle tracks. [Source 1](https://github.com/vocnu/engine/blob/main/README.md)

Voice-over versions of lectures or films, especially the narrator-over-original style. [Source 1](https://github.com/vocnu/engine/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install uv and FFmpeg; use the repository-backed uvx entry point or a source environment. Configure separate OpenAI and ElevenLabs credentials privately.

```sh
uvx --from git+https://github.com/vocnu/engine vocnu-engine translate film.mkv --to lt
```

3. Inspect translation, pronunciation, subtitle timing and the retained original track.

- **Software:** Python 3.12 via uv, FFmpeg; optional Tesseract for image subtitles. Optional CUDA extra accelerates transcription.
- **Platforms:** CLI/Docker workflows documented; a complete operating-system support matrix is not provided.

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Unmarked triplets and difficult handwriting can fail. OCR text is optional and may miss names or titles; author benchmark scores are not independent validation.

### How it uses AI

Learned symbol detectors and a small context transformer convert scanned musical notation into MusicXML. [Source](https://github.com/kobimusic/copisteria/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://huggingface.co/collections/kobimusic/copista-6ac3033f1a779b0c63ad41a9)

Learned symbol detectors and a small context transformer convert scanned musical notation into MusicXML. [Source 1](https://github.com/kobimusic/copisteria/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://huggingface.co/collections/kobimusic/copista-6ac3033f1a779b0c63ad41a9)

Exploring color interpretations of monochrome photographs and restoring selected scan damage. [Source 1](https://github.com/Makistos/colorize/blob/main/README.md)

2. Use the Linux CPU or Windows DirectML release, or install from source with uv and the matching compute extra. Models download on first use.

```sh
uv sync --extra cpu
```


```sh
uv run colorizer-ui
```


```sh
uv run colorizer in/ out/ --model ddcolor --param size=512
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Makistos/colorize/blob/main/README.md)

1. Load a copy of a monochrome photo and compare more than one model.
2. Adjust chroma blend, saturation and temperature without rerunning inference.

- **Software:** Python 3.11+ and uv for source; ONNX Runtime backend. Source-only diffusion extra adds about 3.6 GB weights.
- **Platforms:** Linux x86-64 CPU and Windows 10/11 DirectML releases documented. CoreML is mentioned as a backend but a tested macOS release is not supplied.

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install with uv; run the supplied toy scene before optional Mitsuba/OIDN and browser-export work.

```sh
uv sync
```


```sh
uv run python -m nrp.train --config examples/toy_sphere.json
```


```sh
uv run python -m nrp.torch_backend.train --config examples/toy_sphere_torch.json
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/README.md)

1. Trace and train the toy scene.
2. Inspect predicted versus reference lighting.
3. Follow the linked quickstart for relighting or inverse objectives and retain the documented failure cases.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** A laptop CPU can run the toy example without a GPU. RAM, VRAM and storage minima for larger scenes are not documented.
- **Software:** Python 3.12 through the supplied nix environment or equivalent; numpy/PyTorch via uv. Optional Mitsuba and OIDN; macOS OIDN needs oneTBB.
- **Platforms:** CPU and MPS paths documented with one Apple-silicon reference machine; no CUDA implementation.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT implementation; optional renderers, denoisers and gallery assets retain their own terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT implementation; optional renderers, denoisers and gallery assets retain their own terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute; no paid model service documented.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

An educational implementation with substitutions from the paper. Multi-light additivity and dynamic-geometry recovery still miss stated targets; do not interpret its demos as production readiness. [Source 1](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/bgyss/nrp/blob/main/README.md)

Planning dialogue scenes and comparing paid video-generation attempts with a specific brief. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

Worked scene plans and reported checks on three Kling clips accompany synthetic grading fixtures; complete real clips are not redistributed. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Copy the skill folder into Claude Code, prepare Python/FFmpeg and image libraries, and optionally install faster-whisper for CPU scene transcription. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Copy the skill folder into Claude Code, prepare Python/FFmpeg and image libraries, and optionally install faster-whisper for CPU scene transcription.

```sh
pip install numpy pillow opencv-python
```


```sh
pip install faster-whisper
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

1. Write a scene/keyframe plan and review reference images.
2. Generate the video through the chosen provider and download the take.
3. Inspect cut/line results and lip-sync strips, judge faces by eye and record costs before another attempt.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python 3.10+, FFmpeg/ffprobe, numpy/Pillow/OpenCV, Claude Code; optional faster-whisper and Higgsfield MCP.
- **Platforms:** Helper scripts document Windows, macOS and Linux support.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT skill; Kling/Higgsfield generation, Claude access and downloaded ASR weights remain separately licensed.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT skill; Kling/Higgsfield generation, Claude access and downloaded ASR weights remain separately licensed.
- **Cost:** Local helper compute plus separately billed agent and video/image-generation credits.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Pixel similarity can reward a wrong detail or penalize a correct lighting change; lip sync/identity still require visual judgment. Retimed clips invalidate frame-index drift comparisons. [Source 1](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/joshpocock/shot-director/blob/main/README.md)

### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Apelle333/clipforge/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Several GB for base models and environments; more for NVIDIA runtime and larger ASR models. Sequential execution targets reduced contention on 8 GB GPUs, not a universal minimum.
- **Software:** Python 3.12 recommended/3.11 supported, Node 20+ with 24 tested, FFmpeg full/libass/libx264 and Ollama. Optional NVIDIA driver; CPU mode supported.
- **Platforms:** Windows-first alpha. Docker configurations are supplied but container inference is not claimed as validated.

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Apelle333/clipforge/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/Apelle333/clipforge/blob/main/README.md)

- **Weights:** MIT adaptation retaining upstream AutoClip attribution; Whisper/phi4-mini/MediaPipe weights, NVIDIA runtime and FFmpeg have separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT adaptation retaining upstream AutoClip attribution; Whisper/phi4-mini/MediaPipe weights, NVIDIA runtime and FFmpeg have separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute; no account or paid inference API required by the documented default path.

## ComfyUI

Images & design · Video, animation & film · VFX, compositing & relighting

Substantive v0.43.0 update: MOSS rope/color-depth controls and edit LoRA support, ACEScg/EXR and related image-pipeline work. This extends the October 1 guide; the confidence label retains its explicitly scoped SDXL Base-only meaning.

### Quality assessment: Ready to try

Fresh source review retains Ready to try for the narrow SDXL Base-only workflow. The five non-results checks are supported; source examples do not establish a creative-results pass or a recommendation for newer model integrations.

Scope: Local built-in SDXL Base 1.0 text-to-image using the official Stability AI checkpoint, without refiner, custom nodes, external LoRAs or paid API nodes.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-28. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/blob/master/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Current base-only example and model-card comparisons were reviewed. They do not establish sufficiently scoped creative-result evaluation for a results pass; no application was run. [Source](https://comfyanonymous.github.io/ComfyUI_examples/sdxl/) [Source](https://huggingface.co/stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0)
- **Reproducible setup · verified:** Re-read platform-specific installation, checkpoint placement and the official base-only example. Inspected the independent Krita client implementing checkpoint discovery, graph submission and image receipt. This supports a reproducible implemented route, not a new test on every platform. [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/blob/master/README.md) [Source](https://comfyanonymous.github.io/ComfyUI_examples/sdxl/) [Source](https://github.com/Acly/krita-ai-diffusion/blob/main/ai_diffusion/backend/comfy_client.py)
- **Maintenance and support · verified:** Reviewed five releases, ten commits and ten recently updated issue/PR records. v0.43 includes graph cleanup synchronization and current model fixes; frontend pin/cache fixes are also present. The large-input-directory slowdown is disclosed. This bounded check supports active maintenance of the scoped route without certifying every new node. [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/releases/tag/v0.43.0) [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/issues/17371) [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/pull/17402) [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/pull/17348)
- **Independent use · verified:** Inspected the current third-party Krita integration: it discovers checkpoints, constructs prompt graphs, sends them to ComfyUI and handles generated-image transfers, progress and failures. This is actual maintained implementation evidence rather than a README link or popularity count. [Source](https://github.com/Acly/krita-ai-diffusion/blob/main/ai_diffusion/backend/comfy_client.py)
- **Models, services and costs · verified:** Re-read the complete SDXL Base 1.0 Open RAIL++-M terms and its model card, including standalone-base use, encoder components and use/distribution restrictions. The local base workflow does not require a refiner, custom nodes or a paid API. Other weights and hosted features are outside this assessment. [Source](https://huggingface.co/stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0) [Source](https://huggingface.co/stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0/blob/main/LICENSE.md) [Source](https://comfyanonymous.github.io/ComfyUI_examples/sdxl/) [Source](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/blob/master/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Ready to try applies only to the local base-only SDXL workflow without refiner, custom nodes, LoRAs or paid nodes. Sampled support reports include slow object_info with large input directories and confusion over stable/frontend versions. New MOSS controls have not received an equivalent quality pass.

A node-graph application runs local generative media models. - **Reproducible setup · documented:** The README links a signed APK. Install on a compatible Android device, open API keys and providers, and configure only the speech/translation/voice services you intend to use. A source build needs JDK 17+, Android build tools 34 and platform android-34. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/technicalnihilist-lgtm/dubstudio/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Reviewed a bounded live sample of 10 commits, 5 release records and 0 issue/PR records. Latest sampled commit: 2026-10-28. - **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT app code; Sarvam, OpenAI, ElevenLabs, Gemini and custom endpoints have separate provider terms and charges. Audio/text leave the phone for the selected service, despite local video muxing. Bring provider API keys and available quota/credits; the MIT app does not make the hosted inference free. [Source](https://github.com/technicalnihilist-lgtm/dubstudio/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. No diarization or lip-sync. Center-channel subtraction may leave old speech; long translations can be accelerated. The entire audio track is buffered, so long recordings may exceed Android heap limits. README privacy sections focus on Sarvam while the newer provider table lists additional services.

The README warns that Pillow conversion of 16-bit RGB TIFF can silently reduce depth. Without exiftool, metadata handling is degraded. Review pages and preservation claims do not verify image fidelity.

### How it uses AI

An agent-readable restoration workflow pairs an external image-editing model with Python helpers for source preservation, metadata transfer, provenance and visual review pages. [Source](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

An agent-readable restoration workflow pairs an external image-editing model with Python helpers for source preservation, metadata transfer, provenance and visual review pages. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Organizing scanned-photo restoration with optional identity references and a checkable before/after review record. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

README describes a comparison slider, crops and per-photo verdicts. Claims about identity/metadata preservation are the author’s workflow objectives, not an independent guarantee or a scout runtime test. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install the reviewed skill in a compatible agent or read it as workflow documentation. Helper scripts need Python 3.9+, Pillow and exiftool for full metadata handling; ImageMagick or macOS sips supports HEIC/HEIF, and osxphotos is optional for Apple Photos. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the reviewed skill in a compatible agent or read it as workflow documentation. Helper scripts need Python 3.9+, Pillow and exiftool for full metadata handling; ImageMagick or macOS sips supports HEIC/HEIF, and osxphotos is optional for Apple Photos.

```sh
npx skills add jesserobbins/photo-restore
```


```sh
python scripts/new-project.py grandma --source target.jpg --face face.jpg
```


```sh
python scripts/finalize.py grandma/source/target.jpg render.png --project grandma --out-dir grandma/output
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

1. Create a project using copies/paths for the source and optional references.
2. Use the selected external image model to edit the actual source and save a temporary render.
3. Finalize metadata/provenance, generate the review page and inspect faces, texture and invented details before accepting a result.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python 3.9+, Pillow and optional metadata/format utilities as listed. A compatible image-editing agent/model is separate; this repository bundles no upscaler or face model.
- **Platforms:** Agent/file workflows depend on the chosen host. Apple Photos integration is macOS-only; no complete Windows/Linux helper compatibility matrix is documented.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT workflow/helper code. External agent and image-model access, service terms and photograph rights are separate.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT workflow/helper code. External agent and image-model access, service terms and photograph rights are separate.
- **Cost:** Local helper compute plus any chosen paid agent/image-model access; no dedicated GPU server is required by the helper workflow.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Cannot recover unavailable historical detail. The README warns that Pillow conversion of 16-bit RGB TIFF can silently reduce depth. Without exiftool, metadata handling is degraded. Review pages and preservation claims do not verify image fidelity. [Source 1](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/jesserobbins/photo-restore/blob/main/README.md)

## T2M Mamba

Motion capture & character animation · AI choreography and dance composition · Avatars, digital humans & lip sync

A recently created project receiving its first full library guide; its creation is not proof of production readiness.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Exploring prompt-driven character motions for an animation pipeline.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-28. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The model card describes English prompts yielding HumanML3D or KIT-ML joint arrays for 1–196 frames. The paper’s FID is not a revalidated measurement for these released export hashes; no independent animation demo was established. ### How it uses AI

A diffusion denoiser couples periodic motion and keyframe saliency with language conditioning to generate human-motion features and joint arrays. ### Install

Clone the repository into a separate CUDA environment, install the documented PyTorch/dependency versions and custom scan backend, then download and checksum the weights-v1 archive before extracting it. Run the environment/weight verification helpers before a small generation. [Source 1](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/weights/README.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/MODEL_CARD.md) [Source 5](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md) [Source 6](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/docs/SOURCE_AUDIT.md) [Source 7](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/releases/tag/weights-v1)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the repository into a separate CUDA environment, install the documented PyTorch/dependency versions and custom scan backend, then download and checksum the weights-v1 archive before extracting it. Run the environment/weight verification helpers before a small generation.

```sh
conda create -n t2mmamba python=3.9 -y
```


```sh
conda activate t2mmamba
```


```sh
python -m pip install torch==2.0.1 torchvision==0.15.2 torchaudio==2.0.2 --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu118
```


```sh
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
bash tools/install_backend.sh
```


```sh
python tools/check_environment.py
```


```sh
python tools/verify_weights.py
```


```sh
python -m scripts.generate --opt_path checkpoints/t2m/t2m_condunet1d/opt.txt --text_prompt "A person walks forward and waves." --frames 196 --seed 0 --output_dir outputs/walk
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/weights/README.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/MODEL_CARD.md) [Source 5](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md) [Source 6](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/blob/main/docs/SOURCE_AUDIT.md) [Source 7](https://github.com/chorl0229/T2Mmamba/releases/tag/weights-v1)

1. Install the checksum-verified HumanML3D or KIT-ML inference artifacts.
2. Generate a short English-prompted motion within the documented frame limit.
3. Inspect the exported features/joint arrays and convert them for the chosen animation pipeline; do not treat them as validated robot trajectories.

### TAP AI · MIT

Exploring clickable product overlays without rerendering the base video.

End-to-end segmentation, model provisioning, hardware and output remain unverified; hosted APIs and model terms are separate from MIT code.

### PASE / StuPASE · Apache-2.0

Investigating speech cleanup for recorded dialogue and narration.
Generative speech-enhancement models use a learned phonological prior to reduce unwanted noise while aiming to limit hallucinations.
Full guide pending: the root requirements pin both torch 2.4.1 and 2.1.1, and both torchaudio 2.4.1 and 2.1.1. Subproject inference guides exist, but a consistent install environment, complete weight terms and hardware minima remain unresolved.

### GenASL · GPL-3.0

Researching an accessible signing overlay for video, with substantial unfinished motion and avatar stages.
The implemented audio/LLM planning stages use speech, prosody and language modeling toward a sign-language avatar timeline.

### SyntheticSense · Apache-2.0

Prototyping vision-to-haptic interfaces for accessible interactive installations, with substantial hardware and integration review still needed.
A released YOLOv8 detection path can drive directional vibration and tactile messaging; the inspected code also contains mock camera/detection fallbacks.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Apache-2.0 project code; YOLO/Ultralytics weights and runtime terms are separate. README calls this a research concept with unfinished integration, while current code supplies a YOLO path. MobileNet/IMX500 mock paths must not be confused with real sensing; hardware docs conflict between Pi Zero 2W and Pi 5. No navigation reliability is established.

### HiSTF Mamba · MIT

Investigating text-conditioned motion generation and model training for animation.
A diffusion motion model combines part/whole-body spatial structure, bidirectional temporal modeling and fusion for text-driven human movement.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT author-owned code; upstream Mamba components, HumanML3D/KIT-ML data and evaluator artifacts have separate terms. A matching pretrained checkpoint is unavailable and the reconstructed architecture differs from the paper’s parameter count. Paper FID scores have not been reproduced by this source reconstruction.

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **Modly** · excluded: The complete modified MIT grant adds mandatory visible UI/docs credit. This custom grant is not treated as unmodified MIT or verified OSI licensing. [Source](https://github.com/lightningpixel/modly)
- **NeuralScreen and neural-lens** · excluded: Reviewed PolyForm Strict terms restrict commercial use, modification and distribution; current software does not meet this library’s open-source boundary. [Source](https://github.com/perseval-BLR/NeuralScreen)
- **MRBNN** · excluded: The full reviewed software terms expressly restrict use to non-commercial purposes. Public research code is not sufficient for inclusion. [Source](https://github.com/xiaomi-research/mrbnn)
- **Varve** · excluded: The reviewed FSL-1.1 grant restricts competing uses before a future MIT conversion; a future change date does not make the present release open source. [Source](https://github.com/varvedesign/varve)
- **Heliosonic Sonifier** · excluded: Despite the root GPL label, the README describes a documentation-only repository and a separately proprietary implementation with additional non-commercial restrictions. Eligible runnable open-source software was not established. [Source](https://github.com/HugoPaquete/Heliosonic-Sonifier)
- **NeAR** · needs-license-review: No readable software license was available for the reviewed repository. Neural rendering claims do not replace a verified software grant. [Source](https://github.com/Luh1124/NeAR)
- **Worldmap AI** · excluded: Inspected the actual query engine: country data, aliases and rule-based responses do not establish model inference. Excluded for missing concrete AI implementation, despite its MIT software grant. [Source](https://github.com/karthikrshet/worldmap-ai)
- **Surilo, Mmdance, silkreel and Pars Puppet Studio** · excluded: Reviewed workflows rely on conventional pitch/retargeting/procedural/animation methods. AI-generated inputs, agent-development instructions or marketing names alone do not establish an AI function in the application. [Source](https://github.com/Binamraaa/surilo)
- **Brightbound** · needs-license-review: GitLab search surfaced an AI illustration/book workflow, but direct README and full-license retrieval failed. Retained as unresolved, not admitted from search claims. [Source](https://gitlab.com/Roxanne_Ardary/brightbound)
- **pano-retouch** · excluded: The README still describes a concept-phase application; newer maintenance records report technical spike work. A released or integrated creative AI workflow was not established from this bounded review, so the planned editor was not admitted as an available application. [Source](https://github.com/777555777/pano-retouch)
- **HoloSplat robot digital-twin modules** · excluded: The reviewed repository contains registration/robot mapping and controls around separately supplied reconstructed Gaussian scenes. A distinct learned creative function within these selected modules was not established, so it was not admitted solely because an input scene can come from AI reconstruction. [Source](https://github.com/Cyberyyc/HoloSplat-3DGS-Robot-Digital-Twin)
- **Existing sparse research leads** · needs-license-review: Revisited HFG-Neural-Holo and brain2music; missing released checkpoints or sufficiently complete setup paths leave the existing screened entries pending. Unchanged entries were not recycled as new discoveries. [Source](https://github.com/HannahZhang1926/HFG-Neural-Holo)
- **SubGenPlayer current availability** · needs-license-review: Fresh repository, README, license, release and issue requests returned 404. The historical guide is preserved, while its current library assessment records unknown affected checks. The reason for the missing source is unknown. [Source](https://github.com/megatih/SubGenPlayer)
- **Duplicate, redirect and unavailable repositories** · excluded: The localized SolidCraft candidate redirects to the already profiled upstream; an OpenMontage fork did not establish independent creative functionality. gsstudio-public is an archived redirect and the avatar candidate returned 404. No duplicate or inaccessible implementation was admitted. [Source](https://github.com/tangency-labs/solidcraft)
